1 
台積電可能與馬斯克合作興建並 
營運「Terafab」評析

報告日期： 2025/09/19 研 究 員：羅  金 鳳 (Sunny Lo)  
新 聞 評 析 
新聞重點摘要： 
台積電可能與馬斯克合作興建並營運「 Terafab 」。

一. 我們認為成案可能性極低  
我們認為台積電僅基於商業友好關係與馬斯克進行商討，但成案
的可能性極低，主因台積電表明過其晶圓廠的建置與營運都以
100% 自主擁有為主 。 雖然目前在德國的德勒斯登的ESMC 為合資
(台積電持股 70% 、博世持股 10% 、英飛凌持股 10% 、恩智浦持
股 10%) ，及在日本的熊本廠 JASM 為合資 (台積電持股 86.5% 、
Sony 持股  6.0% 、DENSO 持股 5.5% 、Toyota 持股 2.0%) ，但大
都因 2021 ～2022 年 COVID-19 期間車用晶圓持續供不應求，廠
商為了避免因半導體產能供貨不足而影響出貨的情況發生，紛紛
以能確保半導體晶圓供充足為優先，且後續接續 AI 需求蓬勃發
展，晶圓需求緊俏程度更加嚴重，各國紛紛力邀台積電到該國設
廠，日本及德國為汽車製造大國，因此，台積電於日本及德國的
設廠大都以車用的成熟製程或特殊製程為主 (僅 Sony 以 CMOS 影
像感測為主 )，在當地政府的建廠補貼下，也開放該國企業持有部
份股權。

我們認為本次傳言台積電可能與馬斯克合作興建並營運
「 Terafab 」的技術層次將會拉高到最先進製程的水準，一旦
Terafab 開始營運後，將直接與台積電的先進製程產生衝突，因
此，我們認為成案的可能性極低。馬斯克建置的晶圓廠初期的想
法也是因為晶圓代工產能持續供不應求，馬斯克將建置自有的晶
圓廠滿足旗下 Tesla 車用晶片 、Optimus 機器人 、Dojo 訓練晶片、
AI 晶片、低軌衛星晶片，尤其因目前 AI 的競爭發展白熱化，晶片
的運算能力及設計複雜度都大幅提升，使用最先進的製程節點勢
在必行，因此，馬斯克先前也曾於 2026 年 4 月宣布將與 Intel 合
作 14A 製程，導入 Terafab 進行生產製造，由於 14A 將是 Intel
下一階段最先進的製程，顯見馬斯克對最先進的製程有強烈的需
求，其 Terafab 勢必以發展最先進製程為主，因此，若台積電與
馬斯克合作，勢必也要以其最先進的製程 2nm 為主，甚至下一世
代的 16A ，才能滿足馬斯克的期待，如此，台積電先進製程機密
可能有外洩風險，且若如馬斯克宣稱的 Terafab 初期產能 10 萬片

2 
/月，目前將達 100 萬片 /月，屆時晶圓代工產能將有過剩問題，因
為台積電 2025 年約當 12 吋晶圓的出貨量約 1,500 萬片 ，Terafab
若達 100 萬片 /月(年產能 1,200 萬片 )，將是一個龐大的產能的䆁
出。

目前台積電 2nm+3nm+5nm 佔總營收比重約 66% ，由於 ASP 相
對較高，我們推估其出貨量僅佔總出貨量的 35% ，毛利率也高於
平均水準 (雖然 2nm 仍在進行良率調整，尚未達公司目標，但其於
4Q25 開始量產，僅僅花兩個季度就已經開始賺錢 )，在 AI 需求強
勁的帶動下，台積電也非常極積的擴充 3nm 的產能，甚至將 5nm
的設備改機成 3nm ，顯見得 5nm 以下為台積電最賺錢的製程節
點，一旦與馬斯克合作 5nm 以下的製程節點，製程機密可能外流
的風險將遠高於未來可能接單的利益。考量台積電持續為 AI 浪潮
下的主要受惠者，先進製程、先進封裝同步大幅優於競爭對手，
預估台積電 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 為 108.1/142.76 元，投資建
議為買進，目標價 3141 元(22X2027 年稅後 EPS) 。

Valuation: We continue to use near-term P/E to derive our 12M TP . Our 2027E target 
P/E is updated to 14.1x (vs. previously at 14.7x), still derived from correlation of peers’ 
forward year P/E to avg. EPS YoY (methodology unchanged). With our updated target 
P/E and earnings estimates, our 12M TP is unchanged at NT$487. The target P/E of 14.1x 
is within the company’s historical avg. and avg.-1std forward trading P/E. Maintain 
Neutral.

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Gigabyte 
We are Neutral rated on Gigabyte with a 12-month target price of NT$487. Our TP is 
based on a 14.1x 2027E P/E which is based on PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s share 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery, 2) faster-/slower-than-expected gaming PC growth, and 3) 
faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 24 Sep 2026 close.

Idea
Current debate: Can MediaTek meet Street 2028 forecasts given Intel EMIB-T
supply issue? Intel's EMIB-T substrate supply has been a potential bottleneck for
future TPU v9 ("Humufish") shipments in 2028, making some investors reluctant to
own MediaTek. The issue stems from a supply-demand mismatch. We forecast 3mn
TPU v9 units, as demand from Google (covered by Brian Nowak) remains strong.
However, as discussed in our AI Supply Chain Tracker, we expect only 2mn EMIB-T

substrates, i.e. 40–45k wafer-equivalent EMIB-T capacity in 2028. This aligns with
Digitimes' report that Google is considering CoWoS as a backup for its 2027 TPU on
lagging EMIB-T substrate yields.  For TSMC, supporting CoWoS could also help
regain market share from EMIB-T. Our checks indicate that both MediaTek and
TSMC are engaging to meet customer (Google) demand.
TSMC's willingness to support production increases confidence in our aggressive

2028 EPS forecast: Assuming TSMC CoWoS-L can support 0.5mn TPU v9 units and
EMIB-T can support 2.5mn units, our 3mn-unit target for 2028 appears highly
achievable, with a higher ASP from the CoWoS version. CoWoS-L carries a
structurally higher cost, and the RDL (redistribution layer) interposer connection
requires tape-out of three additional photomask layers. However, our checks also
suggest that Google is willing to pay these incremental costs to secure dual

sourcing, which should help MediaTek sustain its gross margin.
New debate: Who could be the second CSP customer? Investors have asked
whether MediaTek could win Tesla's AI6 edge AI chip program. We believe AI6 is
better suited to GUC, given its 10%–25% margin profile. Considering the higher tech
content of MediaTek's design services, incl. IP + design capabilities, we believe
MediaTek targets a 30%–35% margin profile. Instead, we see a growing probability

that MediaTek wins a cloud AI ASIC project from an AI-focused CSP and/or AI lab
customer through its  Nvidia / NVLink Fusion partnership. In previous notes, we
highlighted interest from both SpaceX and an AI lab customer. The customer's
decision could come in 4Q26 and serve as a significant catalyst for MediaTek. With
TPU upside and a potential new project, we expect MediaTek AI ASIC-related
revenue of US$13.5bn and US$54bn in 2027 and 2028, resp., representing c.38% and

72% of total revenue. Reiterate Top Pick.

### Page 2

Idea
2
TSMC CoWoS capacity support eases concerns regarding
MediaTek 2028  TPU production
Smartphone market still lukewarm into 2027, but can serve as a long-term option
value: At our recent China Best Conference, meetings involving China smartphone OEM
and NAND module makers appeared to indicate China's smartphone demand may not
recover in 2027, as mobile DRAM prices may not be falling anytime soon. We expect
MediaTek’s 3Q26 revenue to be ahead of guidance because of customer pull-in to avoid

the SoC price hike rather than due to demand improvement.
That said, once AI smartphones become a killer application, MediaTek should rank among
the best-positioned semiconductor companies to provide the edge computing capabilities
required in handsets. We also believe 2028 should be a strong year for MediaTek's
smartphone SoC business.
1. Google TPU supply chain update
TSMC CoWoS may  support both 3nm (Zebrafish) and 2nm (Humufish) MediaTek TPU
in 2028: In our earlier report, MediaTek: 2Q26 results preview: Smartphone weak, but

Cloud AI taking off; OW (26 Jul 2026), we noted that  MediaTek may secure a portion of
CoWoS capacity for 2nm TPU to ensure minimum volume production. But, we believe it
will use EMIB-T from Intel (covered by Joseph Moore)  as the primary solution to reduce
costs and scale volume in 2028, if execution proceeds well. Our recent industry checks
suggest TSMC CoWoS would continue to support both 3nm (Zebrafish) and 2nm
(Humufish) MediaTek TPU in 2028. We now assume around 500k units of 2nm TPU going

through CoWoS production as a second source. The 3nm TPU in 2028 of around 2mn
units is higher than our initial expectation of 1mn units. We therefore raise our MediaTek
2028 TPU revenue assumption to US$51.5bn.
2nm TPU TAM is supported by different SKU: At our AI Summit, MediaTek management
stated that its design service is capable of both training and inference design. Recently,
investors have been asking whether MediaTek is involved in the design of 2nm TriggerFish

TPU, intended for AI inference. Our supply chain checks suggest this could be true. The
TriggerFish chip has more features (I/O die upgrade, one more simulation die, and change
of HBM controller to HBM4e), so we think MediaTek’s 2nm TPU blended ASP could go
higher. We estimate ASP at US$12-20k (we now model US$13k). But again, the EMIB-T
substrate yield should be the key risk to volume.
CoWoS constraint eased, but ABF substrate supply still the gating factor for TPU bull

case: In our recent AI supply chain tracker, we noted that MediaTek’s 180k CoWoS-S
booking implies ~3.6mn units of TPU 8t (ZebraFish) in 2027, above our original 3mn
shipment assumption. However, we continue to bake in some conservatism as we had
assumed ABF substrate shortages. So,  we still forecast 3mn units in 2027.

2. Who could be the second and third customer for MediaTek
in AI ASICs?
NVLink Fusion partnership should be the key catalyst for MediaTek to win new
projects:
Through the NVLink Fusion platform, MediaTek's XPU customers can leverage NVIDIA's
scale-up and scale-out networking technologies to build AI server systems.
In our report, MediaTek: NVIDIA and Alphabet Invest in MediaTek’s CB for Extensive
Partnership in ASIC; Stay OW, Top Pick (31 Aug 2026), we shared that MediaTek had

maintained its 2027 ASIC TAM and market share guidance despite the broader partnership,
noting that commercialization would take time. However, we believe the partnership
materially increases MediaTek's chances of winning a second CSP customer.
Overall, we see upside to our 2028 ASIC revenue assumptions, driven by: 1) a higher
probability of winning a second or even third CSP customer; 2) greater revenue from
material preparation; and 3) a strengthened partnership with Alphabet on TPU programs.

In today's research, we incorporate potential revenue contributions from new customers.
Exhibit 2: NVLink Fusion to support connection with custom ASIC
Source: Nvidia
MediaTek's second CSP customer could begin contributing revenue from subsystem
builds in 2028: We believe the subsystem would include procurement of key components
such as HBM and ABF substrates, as well as wafer foundry capacity prepayments.

### Page 4

4
Our checks suggest MediaTek may target subsystem builds for a second CSP customer
rather than supplying the XPU itself. We now expect an initial revenue contribution in
2028, based on approximately 100k subsystem units at an ASP of US$25k.
We therefore assume a US$2.5bn revenue contribution in 2028, based on 100k units and
an ASP of US$25k, including HBM chip consignment, known-good dies (KGDs) for
compute chips, and chiplets.
On margin trends, management highlighted continued value creation for customers across

both silicon and packaging design. As a result, we expect ASICs to remain accretive to
operating margins. At the gross margin level, management indicated that margins vary by
project, potentially reflecting support for full-rack systems. We model 2nm TPU gross
margins at c.35% (vs. c.40% for 3nm TPUs) given the larger revenue scale. We continue to
expect operating margins of ~20–25%, above the smartphone business unit's 15–20%.

3. Why TSMC is motivated  to support MediaTek and Google?
TSMC's CoWoS-L solution carries a structurally higher cost due to its larger reticle size, as
we discussed in The Foundry Floorplan: TSMC preview update; CoWoS vs. EMIB
competition (14 Apr 2026). We believe TSMC also wants to address growing competition
from Intel's EMIB-T, which we estimate will reach 40-45k wafer equivalent capacity 2028,

implying a 10-15% share of the 2.5D packaging market.
For MediaTek to adopt TSMC's CoWoS-L as a second source alongside EMIB-T, the RDL
(redistribution layer) connection to the interposer requires tape-out of three additional
photomask layers. However, our checks suggest that Google is willing to absorb the
incremental costs associated with dual sourcing, which should allow MediaTek to sustain
its gross margin.

Raise price target from NT$5,588 to NT$6,188
We continue to derive our price target (our base-case scenario value) from a residual
income model. The price target change mostly reflects our earnings estimate changes. All
of  our key valuation assumptions remain unchanged: cost of equity at 9.2% (beta of 1.2,
risk-free rate of 2%, and risk premium of 6%), intermediate growth rate of 12%, and
terminal growth rate of 3.0%.
Our bull- and bear-case scenario values rise from NT$7,675 to NT$8,500 and  NT$2,845 to

NT$3,150, respectively.

Morgan Stanley Research 9
Relative valuation
We view the P/E as relatively attractive – even after the share price rally – given strong AI
demand. Although MediaTek faces smartphone headwinds in 2026, we believe it is time to
look beyond 2026. We think this margin pressure is fully priced in, while TPU upside is
not. We believe the stock should trade more than +1SD above its historical average,
supported by strong TPU demand. Our  price target of NT$6,188 implies 42x  2027 EPS, on

our estimates.

to pass through additional costs in Chinese
smartphones.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Pegatron (4938.TW): AI servers in expansion; New smartphone model 
with Broadband entering peak season to support growth ahead; Sell
28 September 2026 | 10:33AM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect sequential revenue growth in Sep-Dec, 
mainly driven by AI server racks ramp up with higher ASP and major smartphone 
brand customer’s new model launches (report link). Overall, we project Pegatron’s 
3Q/4Q26E revenues to grow +18%/+25% YoY, driven by: (1) new model launches 
from a major smartphone customer supporting September and 4Q26 revenue 
growth; (2) a continuing ramp-up in AI server shipments, across rack-level and 
baseboard-based, riding on the growing demand in Neocloud and enterprises, (3) 
broadband, IoT, and game console entering peak season. We are positive on 
Pegatron’s expansion from consumer electronics to AI servers and automotive 
electronics, while the high memory costs could cap PC and smartphone market 
growth. Maintain Sell on stretched valuation.

Pegatron’s Aug revenue was up 34% YoY / 3% MoM to NT$88bn, or 12% lower than 
our estimate. This reflects that high memory costs continue to cap PC and 
smartphone growth, despite a strong ramp-up in AI servers. Aug PC shipments was 
down 49% YoY to 0.40-0.45m, reflecting the impact of consumption pull in under 
the high memory costs. Its major smartphone brand customer launched new models 
in Sep, supporting Pegatron’s growth in Sep and 4Q26; prior to September, the 
company suffered from model transition (i.e. declining demand for older models 
while new models were in a slow shipment ramp-up phase).

Earnings revision: We factor in Pegatron’s Aug revenue and our 2026-28E net income 
largely unchanged. We cut our 2026-28E revenues by 1% / 1% / 1% to reflect the miss in 
consumer electronics under high memory costs. Our 2026-28E GM, OPM and NM are 
largely unchanged.

Valuation: We continue to derive our 12M TP based on near-term P/E, and our target 
P/E is updated to 10.8x 2027E EPS, (vs. 10.6x previously), derived from the refreshed 
peers correlation of forward year earnings growth and P/E.  With the updated earnings 
and target multiple, our 12M TP is revised up to NT$76.9 (vs. NT$75.5 previously). The 
target P/E of 10.8x is within the company’s historical avg. and avg.-1std forward trading 
P/E. Maintain Sell.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

### Page 2

Mediatek (2454.TW)
29 September 2026 Citi Research
2
•MediaTek’s advanced-node and supply chain capabilities are underestimated — We believe MediaTek’s Google
TPU order can scale materially into 2028 and beyond despite market concerns regarding possible project delays. The
TPU V8 chip is already in production, and we believe it is on track for US$2bn revenue in 2026. MediaTek has also
consistently been a top 5 customer at TSMC for the most advanced node with big volumes for several years. They

have multiple N2 projects and early work extending toward A14. This matters because leading-edge ASIC execution
is not only about SerDes, on which MediaTek is also catching up with global peers; the RTL design would require
advanced node design and technology co-optimization, physical implementation, foundry capacity management,
package and substrate coordination, testing, and high-volume ramp. Under Google’s COT/semi-custom model, this
capability should become even more relevant as MediaTek provides design, integration, and manufacturing

execution.
•EMIB-T risk for TPU v9, yet CoWoS/CoPoS for v10 likely to succeed — While investors are also skeptical about
MediaTek’s SerDes technology, we note that its 224G SerDes is in production and that 336G and 448G SerDes is
actively under development. MediaTek is also developing die-to-die, which has already been silicon-validated on
TSMC N3P and N2P. For advanced packaging, we would nevertheless not dismiss EMIB-T yield risk for TPU v9 and

large-scale chiplet integration. Yet we believe there is strong chance that MediaTek will be working on
CoWoS/CoPoS for TPU v10, which is aiming for 2029 production. And its higher value for v9 and long lift v8 should
be more than enough to compensate for the potential shipment downside into 2028.
•Growing with expanding TPU opportunity + new customer engagement — There have been reports that Marvell,
AMD, and Samsung Foundry are potential alternatives for high-end HBM4E inference TPU and future TPU V10

designs. In our view, Google’s expanding TPU TAM should support multiple suppliers and TPU V10 is more likely a
2029–2030 generation. We believe MediaTek and TSMC will continue to be the main source. Given MediaTek’s tight
relationship with Nvidia and the NVLink Fusion ecosystem, we believe it has other hyperscale opportunities such as
Microsoft and xAI.
•Maintaining Buy; more than doubling AI revenue into 2028 from 2027 — In our view, Mediatek is emerging as a

credible hyperscale ASIC partner with materially stronger advanced-node, chiplet, SerDes, and supply-chain
capabilities than the bear case implies. EMIB-T and first-generation large-chiplet execution remain the key risks, but
MediaTek’s rapid learning curve, manufacturing scale, cost discipline, and Google’s capacity-sharing model provide
meaningful mechanisms to mitigate them. We estimate MediaTek’s TPU shipment to be at 4m units for 2027 and
2028, but 2028 revenue to double compared to 2027 due to higher ASP on upgraded SerDes (336G) and N2

advanced node top die design effort.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Short-Term View on Mediatek (2454.TW)
Direction: Upside
Duration: Within 90 Days (expires 30 Oct 2026)

We expect Mediatek's positive guidance on AI ASIC sales outlook to support its share price performance in the
coming months.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 4

Mediatek (2454.TW)
29 September 2026 Citi Research
4
Mediatek
Valuation
Our NT$6,800 target price (rounded) for MediaTek is based on 35x P/E to 2027/2028 EPS, which is 1.5-std above its five-year
average forward P/E given the promising outlook from its AI ASIC business starting from 2H26. We believe our target PE is also
justified by Mediatek's global and regional peers' trading at an average of 36x PER. AI ASIC development with CSPs should
provide more upside from 2027 onwards. We also see Mediatek well prepared for 6G and 6G+NTN Satellite communication.

Despite lukewarm smartphone overall deamand, we expect Mediatek to benefit from 5G migration in emerging markets and its
Dimensity series should also gain shares in flagship models. Non-smartphone business is also seeing stabilizing TV and IoT
demand with Mediatek outgrowing the overall market on its better technology in WiFi 7 and connectivity.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected growth from

AI ASIC business; 2) more severe competition in the smartphone segment; 3) slower overall demand related to macroeconomic
risks, especially for smartphone demand; and 4) further price hikes from foundries.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

ab 29 September 2026
Global Research
Fast Take
ChipMOS Technologies
Headline
ChipMOS posted Aug EPS of NT$0.62, accounting for 49%/46% of UBSe/street
Q3 EPS.
Our Take
Better Aug EPS should be driven by OpM strength rather than non-op. With Aug
sales already reported tracking 69%/68% of UBS/street for 10+% QoQ growth,
and GM likely improving sequentially in Q3, we believe Q3 EPS is tracking above
expectations.

Company Description
ChipMOS is a semiconductor back-end supplier with 45-50% of its sales from display driver
IC (~40% from TV and 40% from smartphone and IT and 20% from automotive), 35-40%
from memory (~40% DRAM, ~40% from specialty flash and ~20% from mainstream NAND)

and 10-15% from logic (TV SoC) and mixed signal IC (MEMS, PMIC).
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We have a Buy rating and a NT$166 price target, based on 23x average 2027-28E PE (vs. its
normalized 5-27x PE range). Our target multiple is in line with the average of memory and
driver IC OSAT peers.Downside risks include a weaker-than-expected tech end-market
demand recovery, growing China competition, failure to diversify into the SiPh/optical

component bumping business and a slower ramp of its logic testing business.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

### Page 3

NYPCB (8046.TW)
30 September 2026 Citi Research
3
BT substrate: improving industrywide supply with sustainable memory demand — In
the near term, while we do observe fading BT demand from smartphone, we also see
robust memory demand likely help offset the weakness in smartphone. T o note that,
NYPCB’s memory sales mix has increased to >60% currently from 30% in the past.
Although the market was disappointed about NYPCB’s muted BT price hike in 4Q26,

we think its BT demand, especially from memory products, is likely to be more resilient
than expected, with potential upsides in 4Q26E or 1Q27E. In the medium term, we also
observe Unimicron and SEMCO (report here) are converting its BT capacity into ABF
one, which would gradually benefit the industrywide BT supply side from 4Q26
onward. More meaningful outflowing BT orders would likely occur after 2Q27 with the
last BT orders placed by customers to Unimicron and SEMCO.

Demand continues to build up; upward earnings revisions; Buy — We see
favorable ABF/BT trend for NYPCB in the upcoming quarters. For its PCB segment
(with demand mostly from non-AI applications now), it seems to be stabilizing in
the past few quarters and likely sees some upsides from new applications. Thus,
we raise our 2026/27 /28E earnings by 2%/8%/6% to factor better demand and
price hikes on supply tightness. We raise our TP to NT$1,700 (30x 2027E EPS) from

NT$1,600 (30x 2027E EPS) and reiterate our Buy rating. We also open 90-day
upside CW for NYPCB, as we expect AI chip production ramp along with price hikes
would drive NYPCB upcoming monthly sales momentum in 4Q26E.

NYPCB (8046.TW)

NYPCB
(8046.TW; NT$ 1340.0; 1; 30 Sep 26; 13:30)
Company description
Established in 1997, Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board (NYPCB) is a technology
subsidiary of the Formosa Plastics Group. Nan Ya PCB's main products
include conventional PCB, HDI, Flip Chip substrate (FCBGA and FCCSP), Wire
Bond Substrate, etc. The company has manufacturing sites in Taiwan and
Kunshan, China.
Investment strategy
We rate NYPCB shares as Buy. We believe ABF/BT should see a solid recovery

in 2026 on robust AI demand: 1) NYPCB is the purest ABF play with a
significant earnings turnaround, driven by growing chip sizes and layer counts
for AI ASIC/networking; and 2) the BT business should benefit from price
hikes on rising memory demand and T glass shortage. Considering T glass
support from NYPCB’s parent company, we believe NYPCB would  have a
better chance to win additional AI ASIC/networking business going forward.
Valuation
Our DCF-based TP for NYPCB is NT$1,700. With a risk-free rate of 5.00% (10-

year government bond rate), a market risk premium of 7% and an equity beta
of 1.22 (YTD weekly share price), we calculate NYPCB's WACC as 13.4%. Our
TP is equivalent to 30x/13x 2027E EPS/BVPS, which reflects its high P/E
multiple during upcycles. NYPCB traded at 15-20x P/E during its 2003-2008
upcycle driven by PC/NB demand strength. In 2010-11 when the market was
recovering from the global financial crisis, NYPCB traded at 25-35x P/E. In the
last 2019-22 upcycle when ABF was in shortage, NYPCB traded at 30-40x

P/E.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the NYPCB shares from reaching our
target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected ABF demand from key
customers; 2) BT business seasonality weakness and fewer-than-expected
price hikes; and 3) severe T glass shortages that cause a bottleneck to its
production.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

buy recommendation and a Catalyst Watch/STV Downside call corresponds to a sell recommendation. Any stock not assigned

E Ink (8069.TWO): E-paper application continues to expand; expect 
modest consumer electronics mix; Aug beat; Neutral
30 September 2026 | 3:59PM HKT
We remain positive on growing e-paper applications across e-readers, e-notes, and 
e-labels. However, consumer electronics end-demand could remain modest amid 
high memory costs. We expect E Ink’s consumer electronics (e-readers, e-notes) to 
decline 13% YoY in 2026E (vs. +6% YoY in 2025), and recover back to +12-16% YoY 
in 2027E-28E; on IoT applications (ESL and Signage), we expect revenue to grow at a 
+32% CAGR in 2026E-28E (vs. +22% YoY in 2025), driven by rising adoption on 
savings of labour costs, electricity, and paper, digitalization trend, and growing ESG 
awareness. Signage new products could also drive revenue growth with high ASPs, 
despite lower GMs considering in module format to accelerate customers’ adoption. 
Our target P/E multiple declines to 11x (vs. 13x previous TP implied 2027E P/E) on 
consumer electronics sector de-rating under high memory costs. Our new 12m TP is 
NT$142 (vs. NT$153 previously). Maintain Neutral.

3-month revenues preview: We expect a sequential increase in revenues through 
Sep-Dec, driven by e-labels, Signage ramp up, and better consumer electronics 
seasonality. The ongoing digitalization should support e-paper adoption; E Ink’s 
e-labels further penetrated in Canada, serving Sobeys (retail) to deploy ESLs across 
300 to 350 stores over the next 18 months (link). Overall, we expect 3Q26E / 4Q26E 
revenues to grow at +0% / +16% QoQ on the mix of modest consumer electronics 
end-demand under high memory costs, and ongoing e-labels adoption and Signage 
shipment ramp up.

### Page 2

Earnings revision

We factor in 2Q26 and Aug results and revise up our 2026E-28E net income by 15% / 
10% / 8% mainly on higher gross margins, given better product mix (e-labels mainly 
delivered via e-paper while consumer electronics mainly via module, carry a lower GM). 
Overall, we expect 2026E revenues growth at +13% YoY (vs. +12% YoY in 2025A), driven 
by e-labels growing application and adoption, which we model to grow +47% YoY (vs. 
+22% YoY in 2025A), while we also expect a decline in consumer electronics under high 
memory costs (-13% YoY vs. +6% YoY in 2025). We continue to expect revenue growth in 
2027E-28E driven by (1) rising color e-notes / e-readers enlarging size, (2) more stores 
adopting e-labels to save operation costs with growing outdoor signage. On GM, we 
increase our forecasts in 2026E-28E to reflect 2Q26 beat on better product mix.

### Page 3

Valuation

We continue to derive our 12m TP based on a near-term P/E. We derive our target P/E 
via E Ink’s avg. 12m forward PEG ratio since 2017. With largely the same PEG ratio of 
0.5x, 22% forward year earnings growth (vs. 26% previously), our new target P/E is at 
11x (vs. 13x our TP implied 2027E P/E previously). The new target P/E of 11x is largely in 
line with the company’s avg.-1stv. P/E, reflecting the consumer electronics sector 
de-rating under high memory costs. Our new 12m target price is NT$142 (vs. NT$153 
previously). Maintain Neutral.

### Page 4

Price target methodology and risks - E Ink 
Valuation methodology: We are Neutral rated on E Ink. Our 12m target price for E Ink is 
based on a near-term P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. Our 12-m 
TP of NT$142 is based on a target P/E multiple of 11x forward year EPS (2027E). Our 
target P/E multiple of 11x is derived from the company’s avg. PEG since 2017 (0.5x), as 
we expect macro uncertainties to reduce the visibility of e-paper adoption, while we 
remain positive in the long term: technology upgrades (colour, larger sizes) to unlock the 
e-paper market potential. 
Upside / Downside risks: (1) Faster- / slower-than-expected e-readers / e-notes 
penetration rate, (2) Faster- / slower-than-expected ESL adoption rate, (3) Fiercer- / 
healthier-than-expected competition, which could be driven by competition from other 
technology gadgets (e.g. AI / AR glasses, AI PCs, tablets, smart watches, etc.) or new 
entrants in e-paper industry.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

調整到 73 元(0.65 X2027 BVPS)。

陽明為全球第九大海運業者：陽明海運是全球第九大航商，2026/9 運力 77.3 萬
TEU 約 99 艘(較 2025 年底的 71.4 萬 TEU 成長 8.3%)，市佔率 2.2%，加入 THE 
Alliance 聯盟 (聯盟成 員之 一赫伯羅特2025/1 退出 ，聯盟更名為 Premier 
Alliance(2025/2-2030/1)。安裝脫硫塔比重超過 6 成，自有櫃佔 54%。1H26 年
美洲線佔營收比重 36%，歐洲線佔 39%，近洋佔 13%，其他航線(中東、南美等)
佔 12%，運送量 223.6 萬 TEU(+4%YoY)，平均運價 1012 美元/TEU(-5%YoY)。
股權方面，官股持有 31%。 
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 為 5.37 元：受惠美洲線與亞洲線運價上漲，加上 LNG 船投
入地中海航線 ， 預估營收656 億元(+56%YoY)， 稅後獲利187.5 億元(+210%YoY)，
稅後 EPS 為 5.37 元。4Q 歐洲線進入淡季、美洲線受巴拿馬運河水位降低相對有
撐、亞洲進入旺季加上塞港相對強勢，公司認為4Q 呈現緩步修正，研究部預估營
收 573 億元(+53%YoY)，稅後獲利 136.4 億元(+496%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 3.91
元。 
燃油成本的影響：陽明 1H26 年燃油成本佔 16%，6 月油價攀升至 692 美元/噸，
整體燃油成本較去年同期+1%。2Q 高硫油平均 617 美元/噸(+23.5%QoQ)，低硫
油平均 775 美元/噸(+24.5%QoQ)，因地緣政治影響，3Q 高硫油平均 562 美元/
噸-9%QoQ)，低硫油平均 792 美元/噸(+2.3%QoQ)，研究部假設陽明年耗油量
142 萬噸，船用燃油平均一年漲 100 美元/噸，燃油成本增加 44 億元，EPS 影響
1.26 元，不過航商透過燃油附加費的徵收以分散燃油成本上漲的壓力。

2026 年供給成長是近幾年最低一年：巴拿馬運河當局 9/4 將每天通行艘樹從 36
艘降至 34 艘，9/15 再降到 32 艘，目前運河水位較去年低 2.5%，預期 10 月水位
將低於去年同期 3.8%，艙位供給將再度限縮。無論是 Drewry，Clarkson 或是
Alphaliner 預估 2026 年船舶供給成長率是近幾年最低的 ， 加上紅海改道的船舶回
歸延遲，有效運力釋放再度放緩，2026 年是供給相對較小的一年。

DWT 散裝船。

Key Data  _____________________________________

GS Forecast  __________________________________

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

TSMC will host its 3Q26 analyst meeting on 15 October. We expect a 
strong finish to 2026 with a sustained growth outlook into 2027 for 
TSMC as demand broadens across AI GPUs/AI ASICs, networking 
and server CPUs. In particular, the stronger CPU demand driven by 
agentic AI represents a key change over the past year. We forecast 
2026E/2027E revenue growth of 42.0%/36.9% YoY (USD terms), 
with GM at 67.2%/67.5%. We also raise our 2027E/2028E CapEx 
forecasts to US$85bn/US$98bn (vs. US$78bn/US$82bn 
previously) as we expect TSMC to further ramp up its capacity 
investment to support longer-term customer demand. 
Strong sequential growth into 4Q26 with modest N2 ramp dilution 
We expect TSMC’s 3Q26E revenue to grow 15.3% QoQ, followed 
by a further 11.0% QoQ increase in 4Q26E (both in USD terms), 
supported by continued strength in AI/HPC demand. On 
profitability, we model GM edging down to 67.5%/67.3% in 
3Q26E/4Q26E, vs. 67.7% in 2Q26, reflecting dilution from the 
ongoing N2 ramp.  
Momentum sustaining into 2027, supported by broader AI demand 
We expect TSMC’s revenue to grow 36.9% YoY (vs. 32.0% previously) 
in USD terms in 2027E, supported by sustained demand for AI 
accelerators and networking, alongside an increasingly strong 
contribution from server CPUs. At our Communacopia + Technology 
conference, management highlighted that lower token costs should 
stimulate greater AI consumption, while discussions with customers 
and US CSPs has reinforced confidence in demand and 
infrastructure readiness. We believe these trends support continued 
strength in leading-edge demand, particularly for both N2 and N3, 
where management expects supply to remain insufficient despite 
further expansion.

### Page 3

On the supply side, we expect capacity additions, N5-to-N3 conversion, and 
productivity gains to support higher wafer output, while continued CoWoS expansion 
helps enable associated wafer shipments. We model GM improving modestly to 67.5% in 
2027E (vs. 67.2% in 2026E), supported by higher pricing, favorable product mix, 
sustained high utilization and continued productivity gains. 
Higher CapEx to support longer-term expansion 
We maintain our 2026E CapEx forecast at US$64bn, while raising our 2027E/2028E 
forecasts to US$85bn/US$98bn (vs. US$78bn/US$82bn previously), to incorporate 1) 
cost inflation from equipment vendors, and 2) initial spending associated with the 
potential new Texas expansion; however, we expect the new site to only enter mass 
production beyond 2032. For capacity, we continue to model our end-2027E/2028E 
N3/N2 capacity at 200kwpm/140kwpm and 220kwpm/200kwpm, respectively. 
Other key areas in focus for the upcoming analyst meeting 
Long-term AI growth outlook: We believe the broadening of AI demand across 
accelerators, CPUs and networking further supports TSMC’s long-term growth 
opportunity. We would look for updated comments from management on the key 
assumptions underpinning its AI outlook through 2030, where demand drivers have 
strengthened most over the past year, and the factors that could materially change this 
outlook. 
Longer-term CapEx outlook and US expansion: With customer demand continuing to 
drive investment in leading-edge capacity and advanced packaging, we would look for 
management to provide further direction on its longer-term CapEx trajectory and the 
demand visibility supporting these investment decisions. We would also welcome 
updated comments on whether customer demand could lead to further expansion in the 
US beyond current Arizona site, and the potential timing and scale of such investments. 
Foundry competition and technology leadership: We would look for management’s 
perspective on the evolving competitive landscape, including Intel, Samsung and 
Terafab, and the implications for TSMC’s longer-term positioning. In particular, we would 
welcome further discussion of its technology leadership, and whether management 
expects this gap to widen or narrow across upcoming process generations.

### Page 5

Earnings estimate revisions, valuation and risks

Forecast changes 
We have revised up 2026/27/28E earnings by 1%/7%/8% to reflect 1) higher GM 
assumptions as we see a milder GM dilution from N2 ramp, and 2) higher UTRs especially 
for N2/N3 given stronger demand from AI/HPC.

Reiterate Buy; TP raise to NT$3,300 from NT$3,100 
We raise our 12m TP to NT$3,300 from NT$3,100 (2330.TW), following our upward 
earnings revisions. Our TP is based on a target P/E of 22x (unchanged, benchmarked to 
1.0stdv above 5-year avg. forward P/E) applied to our higher 2027E EPS. Accordingly, we 
raise our 12 month TP for the ADR to US$660 from US$620, based on an unchanged FX

### Page 7

Investment Thesis - TSMC (2330.TW/TSM) 
TSMC is a leading global foundry company specializing in cutting-edge nodes. Its leading 
technology stance enables it to enjoy more than 60% revenue share in the global 
foundry market. We like TSMC as we believe its solid technology leadership and 
execution better position it vs. peers to capture the industry’s long-term structural 
growth opportunities, particularly in areas such as AI/5G/HPC/EV. In addition, valuation 
looks attractive in our view, with the shares trading at the mid-range of their 10-year 
trading history (P/E: 10-29x). Furthermore, we view TSMC as the key AI enabler among 
our Taiwan Semis coverage, thanks to its leadership stance in leading edge nodes and 
advanced packaging technology – CoWoS (chip on wafer on substrate). We believe TSMC 
will achieve its 25% revenue CAGR target for the next several years, driven primarily by 
increasing silicon content growth and AI/HPC demand, with LT GM to remain at 56%+. 
We are Buy rated.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - TSMC (2330.TW/TSM) 
Valuation methodology: We have a 12m TP of NT$3,300, which is derived by applying a 
target P/E multiple of 22x to our 2027E EPS. Our 22x target P/E is benchmarked against 
1.0stdv above its 5-year trading average. For the ADR (TSM), we have a 12m TP of 
US$660, based on a USD/TWD rate of 30.0 and an ADR premium of 20%. 
Key downside risks to our views: (1) further deterioration in end-demand recovery 
impacting capacity utilization; (2) slower customer node migrations; (3) slowdown in AI 
investment resulting in lower long-term semiconductor content growth; (4) poor 
yields/execution resulting in worse-than-expected profitability; (5) stronger 
competition resulting in ASP/profitability erosion; and (6) unfavorable FX trend or 
higher-than-expected cost increase weighing on the margin outlook.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

- 1 - 
個股報告 
嘉晶(3016) 
8 吋 Si Epi 滿載支撐獲利，Ge/Si 擴產加速 
投資評等 目標價 
區間操作 $152 
前次投資建議：2026.04.30 
「區間操作」 ，目標價: $92

報告日期：2026.09.23 研 究 員：李彝安

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 嘉晶 26Q2 毛利率 23.5%優於預期，8吋 Si Epi 維持滿載，Ge/Si需求能見度延伸至 2028 年，
上修 2026/27F EPS 至 2.56/3.69，目標價 152 元(6.5x2027(F)PBR)，潛在上漲空間 14.7%。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/09/22 收盤價： 132.50 ➢ 26Q2 營收 13.09 億元，季增 23.6%、年增 37.1%，毛
利率 23.5%、營益率16.9%，稅後淨利1.88 億元、EPS 
0.65 元。營收僅略高於研究部原估12.92 億元，毛利率
較原估 18.4%高出 5.1ppt，主要反映8吋產品占比提高
及稼動率改善，因此上修獲利。 
➢ 26H1 8 吋 Si Epi 產能滿載，8吋占 Si Epi 營收比重由
去年同期 59%提升至 71%，MOSFET 與 PMIC 晶圓產
出年增 50%。既有產能仍無法滿足客戶需求，2026 年
新增設備預計自 2027 年陸續開出。 
➢ Compound Epi 營運改善，26H1 GaN/SiC營收分別年
增 20%/28%；6 吋 GaN 已轉虧為盈且產能滿載，SiC
則受惠日本 IDM 客戶自 26Q2 開始放量，稼動率提高
後虧損明顯收斂。 Ge/Si PD Epi 26Q2 營收季增
113%，26H2 營收預估較 26H1 成長 2.5 倍，2027 年
產能預計增至目前 3.5～4倍，訂單能見度延伸至2028
年；惟 26H1 營收占比僅約 1%，研究部推估 2027 年
占比仍約 4%～5%，短期 EPS貢獻有限。 
➢ 研究部上修 2026 年營收至 53.3 億元、毛利率 23.1%、
EPS 2.56元；2027 年營收 68.7 億元、毛利率25.1%、
EPS 3.69元 。 考量8吋 Si Epi維持滿載 、Compound Epi
獲利改善及 Ge/Si後續成長，給予 6.5x 27F PBR，目
標價 152 元。6.5x 已位於公司過去約 2–8x PBR 區間
偏高位置，反映目前獲利改善及成長能見度提升，評價
進一步上修空間有限；潛在上漲空間 14.7%，維持「區
間操作」評等。

資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析 
嘉晶為國內少數同時跨足矽磊晶、化合物半導體與矽光子之專業磊晶代工廠 
嘉晶（3016）成立於 1996 年，總部位於新竹科學園區，為國內專業半導體
磊晶 （Epitaxy） 代工廠，主要股東為漢民科技集團。公司早期以矽磊晶 （Silicon 
Epi）代工起家，產品應用於電源管理 IC（PMIC）、MOSFET 等功率半導
體領域；中期切入化合物半導體磊晶，建置氮化鎵（GaN）與碳化矽（SiC）
產線，跨入射頻與功率元件市場，下游應用涵蓋資料中心電源、電動車、再
生能源、人形機器人與航太科技；近期則進一步切入矽光子（ Silicon 
Photonics）用 Ge on Si（鍺在矽上）光偵測器（PD）磊晶代工，為國內少
數同時具備三大磊晶平台量產能力之專業業者。 
公司於台灣設有 4 吋、5 吋、6 吋、8 吋多尺寸磊晶產線，磊晶製程涵蓋  
Silicon、GaN、SiC、Ge on Si 等多種材料系統，具備磊晶配方設計、設備
運轉、品質與良率管控之完整 Know-How。客戶結構上，下游涵蓋 Fabless 
設計業者與 IDM 廠，訂單來源以台灣為主，日本與其他地區次之。

圖一、嘉晶(3016) 2026H1產品別營收比重

資料來源：嘉晶，兆豐投顧彙整

- 3 - 
個股報告 
26Q2 毛利率優於預期，上修 2026年獲利 
嘉晶 26Q2 營收 13.09 億元，季增 23.6%、年增 37.1%，毛利率 23.5%，季
增 5.7ppt，營益率 16.9%，稅後淨利 1.88 億元、EPS 0.65 元；26H1 營收
23.68億元 、 年增27.9%， 毛利率20.9%，EPS 1.00元 ， 較去年同期EPS -0.14
元明顯改善。 
26Q2營收與研究部原估 12.92億元差異有限 ， 惟毛利率23.5%較原估 18.4%
高出 5.1ppt，獲利優於預期主要來自產品組合及稼動率改善。26H1 持續將
小尺寸產能轉往 8 吋，8 吋產品占比提高，加上 8 吋產線維持滿載，帶動固
定成本吸收及整體獲利改善。26Q2 毛利率尚未反映漲價效益，價格調整自
26Q3 才開始，26H2 將陸續反映效益。  
研究部將 2026 年營收由前次 51.0 億元上修至 53.3 億元，年增 37.0%；毛
利率由 18.1%上修至 23.1%，稅後淨利 7.6億元、EPS由 1.70 元上修至 2.56
元。預估 26Q3/26Q4 營收 14.59/15.06 億元，季增 11.5%/3.2%，毛利率
24.6%/24.9%，EPS均為 0.8元。

8吋 Si Epi維持滿載，2027年新增產能陸續開出 
Si Epi 仍為嘉晶主要營收與獲利來源，應用涵蓋 PMIC、Power MOSFET、
IGBT、Driver 及 Protection IC 等功率元件。AI 伺服器功率密度提升，帶動
PMIC與 Power MOSFET 需求增加，26H1 8吋 Si Epi產能滿載，營收占 Si 
Epi比重由去年同期 59%提升至 71%，MOSFET與 PMIC晶圓產出年增50%。 
國內部分晶圓廠原先具備自有 Epi產能，但AI 相關 PMIC需求增加後，既有
產能不足，帶動委外需求增加。26H2 Si Epi 營收預估年增約 42%，8 吋既
有產能滿載後仍無法滿足客戶需求，目前客戶持續要求擴產，2027 年新增產
能開出後仍為主要營收成長來源。 
AI伺服器功率需求亦帶動不同材料平台成長，其中Si Epi主要對應 PMIC 與
MOSFET；800V高壓直流架構則增加 GaN及 SiC等化合物半導體的應用機
會 。 三項產品雖均受惠AI電源需求 ， 但目前對嘉晶獲利的貢獻仍以8吋 Si Epi
為主。

26Q3 開始調價，26H2營收與獲利優於 26H1 
原物料及相關成本上升，自 26Q3 開始針對不同產品與客戶調整價格。由於
價格調整自 26Q3 才開始 ，26Q2 毛利率由 17.8%提升至 23.5%主要仍反映 8
吋占比及產能利用率改善，而非 ASP上升。26H2 Si Epi 需求維持高檔，加
上價格調整開始反映，研究部預估26Q3/26Q4 毛利率分別為 24.6%/24.9%，
高於 26Q2 的 23.5%。26H2 營收 29.65 億元，較 26H1 成長 25.2%，2026
全年營收 53.3 億元、年增 37.0%，符合公司全年營收年增>35%的展望。 
2027 年毛利率預估進一步提升至 25.1%，主要反映產品組合持續改善、
Compound Epi 獲利回升，以及 Ge/Si較高毛利產品占比增加；另一方面，
2026 年新增設備的折舊將自 2027～28年陸續認列，部分抵銷產品組合改善
效益，因此並未假設 Si Epi毛利率持續大幅上升。

圖二、嘉晶(3016)各季營收及獲利走勢預估

資料來源：Cmoney；兆豐投顧預估

Compound Epi營運改善，GaN轉盈、SiC虧損收斂 
26H1 Compound Epi 營運明顯改善，GaN營收年增 20%、SiC年增 28%。
GaN 自 25Q3 至 26Q2 連續四季成長，6 吋產能目前已滿載並由虧轉盈，8
吋則開始小量生產；現有 6 吋需求高於可供產能，客戶有對公司提出擴產需
求。GaN 後續擴產狀況須配合客戶產品平台及下游 Foundry 進度。目前 6
吋訂單充足，但長期產業逐步往 8吋發展，因此新增產能將依客戶與Foundry
合作成熟度調整。SiC 方面，日本 IDM客戶自 26Q2 開始放量，帶動產能利
用率回升，原先投資虧損明顯縮小。中國SiC市場仍面臨較激烈的價格競爭，
目前嘉晶業務以日本及其他非中國市場為主。

26H2 Compound Epi 營收預估年增 86%，改善幅度高於 Si Epi。惟現階段
Compound Epi營收規模仍低於 Si Epi，2026～27年對獲利的主要貢獻在於
GaN由虧轉盈及 SiC虧損持續收斂，而非取代 Si Epi成為主要獲利來源。

Compound Epi 營運改善，GaN轉盈、SiC虧損收斂 
Ge/Si PD Epi主要應用於高速光通訊接收端 Photodiode，目前對應800G 及
1.6T 可插拔式光模組。26Q2 營收季增 113%，26H2 營收預估較 26H1成長
約 2.5 倍。。需求端能見度已延伸至 2028 年，目前產能只能滿足既有客戶
不到 50%的需求。2027 年產能預計增至目前 3.5～4倍，但依現有訂單需求
仍不足，後續仍有追加設備空間；主要擴產限制來自設備交期。

Ge/Si成長率雖高，但短期對整體EPS貢獻有限。26H1營收占比僅約 1%，
依 26H2 成長幅度及 2027 年 3.5～4 倍成長假設推估，2027 年營收約 3 億

元，占研究部 2027F 營收 68.7 億元約 4%～5%。即使 Ge/Si 毛利率明顯高

擴產效益自 2027年陸續反映，獲利延續成長 
研究部預估 2026 年營收 53.3 億元、年增 37.0%，毛利率 23.1%、營益率
16.7%，稅後淨利 7.60 億元、EPS 2.56 元；2027 年營收 68.7 億元、年增
28.8%，毛利率 25.1%、營益率 19.1%，稅後淨利 10.95 億元、EPS 3.69
元。 
2026 年獲利上修主要反映 26Q2 毛利率優於預期、26H2 8 吋 Si Epi 維持高
稼動率及 26Q3 開始調價；2027 年則受惠 8 吋新增產能陸續開出、GaN 獲
利增加與 SiC 虧損收斂，以及 Ge/Si營收占比提升。 
估值延續採用 PBR評價。嘉晶過去 PBR約落在 2～8x，考量 8吋 Si Epi 供
需改善、Compound Epi 營運轉佳及 Ge/Si中長期成長動能，給予 6.5x 27F 
PBR。6.5x 位於歷史區間偏高位置，已反映部分基本面改善與 2027 年成長
預期，後續評價倍數上修空間相對有限。上調目標價至 152 元(6.5x 27 (F) 
PBR)，潛在上漲空間 14.7%，維持「區間操作」評等。

台勝科 (3532 TT)  Trading Buy

營運逐步回升，建議 Trading Buy 。  
投資建議 ：台勝科為日商 SUMCO(3436 JP) 、台塑 (1301 TT) 、亞
太投資合資成立的專業 8 吋及 12 吋矽晶圓製造商，資本額 38.8 
億元。先前 SUMCO 處份部份台勝科，目前持有股權約 38% ，
台塑集團及其轉投資子公司亞太投資合計持有 38%。AI 需求推
動資料中心建置，相關 12 吋先進製程用晶圓需求強勁，公司持
續擴大銷售 12 吋先進製程矽晶圓、導入 SUMCO 12 吋先進製
程矽晶圓技術並與客戶合作開發高階製程應用，朝高階邏輯矽
晶圓布局。也布局 CoWoS 先進封裝、 3D 封裝與 HBM 矽晶圓。
預估台勝科 2026 年稅後 EPS 2.19 元。整體矽晶圓受惠高階製
程和 DRAM 矽晶圓需求佳，再加上漲價效應，有望帶動營運逐
步向上，建議 Trading Buy ，目標價 489 元 (2027 年 PER65X) 。  
2Q26 稅後 EPS 0.12 元：受惠於 AI 算力基礎建設推動記憶體與
先進邏輯製程需求，客戶產能利用率維持高檔， 2Q26 營收為
36.10 億元， QoQ+9.11% ， 12 吋矽晶圓佔營收比重 >70% 、其餘
為 8 吋矽晶圓。 8 吋矽晶圓主要客戶為晶圓代工，因 AI 伺服器
帶動電源管理 IC 與驅動 IC 補貨需求，產能利用率已達 90% 以
上。毛利率方面，雖因新廠折舊與營運成本上升壓力，但公司
正積極與客戶溝通價格調漲機制，此外，現貨價格回升， ASP 上
揚 ， 產能利用率改善 ，2Q26 毛利率增加 6.06 個百分點至 6.90%，
2Q26 後 EPS 為 0.12 元，轉虧為盈。  
麥寮新廠客戶認證將在 2026 年內完成： 台勝科 12 吋麥寮新廠
總投資額為新台幣 282.6 億元，已於 2025 年底完工。預估 2026
全年資本支出為 33 億元，包含新廠尾款約為 10.27 億元，新廠
設備調整，節能改善工程以及例行性設備汰舊換新。新廠營運
初期處於產能提升與客戶認證階段，較高的新廠折舊攤提費用
(2025 年平均 5.2 億 /季， 1H26 平均 8.5 億 /季 )，疊加人工與原物
料成本的攀升，對公司短期的毛利率與營業利益造成壓抑。不
過麥寮 12 吋新廠產能目前已被客戶預約完售，預估隨著客戶認
證逐步於 2026 年內完成，將擴大對高階產品與記憶體客戶的供
應，推升中長期營收成長以抵消折舊成本的不利因素。

2 群益投顧

台勝科 ESG ：  
公司 ESG 治理架構  
專責永續小組：台塑勝高已成立「企業社會責任推動小組」，由總經理田
中恵一擔任總召集人，副總經理擔任管理代表，專責規劃與推動公司 ESG
相關項目。

永續發展委員會：自 2022 年起設永續發展委員會，制定 ESG 策略並追蹤
目標進度，與全球供應商協作推動碳排減量及永續轉型。

董事會監督： ESG 重大主題及進展每年彙整後提報董事會，由董事會確認
與利害關係人需求一致， 2024 年 6 月最近一次審查。

主要 ESG 成績與目標  
環境（ Environment ）  
減碳目標：承諾 2025 年減碳 12.5% 、 2030 年減碳 25% ，邁向 2050 年碳中
和。

氣候管理：落實溫室氣體盤查及公告，持續推動低碳製程、提高循環利用
與改善能資源效率。

污染防制：積極優化廢水處理、加強空氣污染防制措施，以減少對地方社
區的環境影響。

社會（ Social ）  
員工照顧：推動職安維護、健康促進與育才培訓，強調性別平等與多元融
合。

社區共融：積極參與地方公益、辦理社區關懷與教育回饋計畫，提升企業
社會形象。

供應鏈共識行動： 2022 年起攜手全球 79 家供應商共宣 ESG 行動，打造綠
色供應鏈。

公司治理（ Governance ）  
治理透明：設立 ESG 委員會與內部稽核制度，跨部門協作永續目標，定期
公開永續績效報告。  
誠信經營：強調合規、資訊揭露與企業誠信，增進利害關係人信任。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

上品 (4770 TT) 
在手訂單金額創高，營運轉折向上

永豐觀點 
上品接單表現顯著好轉，在手訂單金額回升至 75 億元，公司產能滿載，產品組合
預期持續改善，公司營運轉折向上，研究部轉趨正向看待上品獲利表現。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議上調至買進，主要係因：上品在手訂單金額自年初約 38 億元提升至 75
億元，為歷史新高，反映台灣及中國地區接單表現明顯好轉，考量公司隨著低毛利
訂單出貨完畢，產品組合應逐步好轉，帶動營運轉折向上，基此，研究部預估公司
2026/2027 稅後 EPS 14.3/21.6 元，參考歷史 PER 區間約位於 10-20X，以 2027
年預估 EPS，給予 16X PER 評價，目標價 345 元，投資建議上調至買進。 
上品企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B+等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

### Page 2

2

上品為從事氟素樹脂材料相關製品之生產廠商：公司成立於 1981 年，主要經營氟
素樹脂材料（鐵氟龍）相關製品，包括氟素原料加工之半成品及成品生產及銷售，
下游應用廣泛，是全球氟素樹脂加工應用廠商中少數能同時滿足各階段設備、製
造、研發、創新、設計等完整服務的企業。2Q26 產品營收比重，氟素內襯設備營
收占 68.3%，氟素內襯管件類占 9.6%，氟素應用材料類占 16.7 %，氟素工程收入
占 5.4%；2Q26 公司產品依應用類別區分，半導體占營收 91.3%，化工應用占比
為 6.4%，面板及太陽能應用占 1.2%，其他應用占 1.1%，2Q26 公司營收按地區
別區分，台灣占 49.3%，中國占 44.8%，美國占 3.4%，其他亞洲占 1.0%，歐洲
占 1.5%。 
2Q26 獲利逐步回升：上品 2Q26 營收 13.05 億元（+23.2% QoQ，-0.6% YoY），
毛利率 35.62%（+0.68pts. QoQ，-1.74pts. YoY） ，稅後EPS 3.11 元。上品2Q26
營收回升，主因公司產能利用率提升，且公司因在手訂單金額回升，於 4M26 進
行產能擴充 ， 帶動營收表現增溫 ， 惟公司於2025 因市況不佳 ， 為填補產能利用率 ，
承接較多低毛利訂單 ， 影響公司毛利率表現受到壓抑 ， 但隨著2Q26 產能利用率拉
升，及製成品出貨帶來存貨評價損失回沖，推升毛利率表現已較1Q26 好轉，預期
隨著公司低毛利訂單陸續出貨完畢，及產能利用率拉升，2H26 獲利應持續好轉。

### Page 3

3

在手訂單金額已顯著回升 ：上品在手訂單金額自年初約 38 億元 ， 至年中提升至60
億元，而至 8 月底已提升至 75 億元，為歷史新高水準，其中，約7 成為台灣地區
訂單，約 3 成為中國地區訂單，美國及其他地區訂單佔比則低於 1 成。2026 接單
槽車數量達 1,000 台以上，優於往年約 600 台水準，反映台灣化學品廠因應晶圓
廠大客戶需求而擴充產能， 對於上品內襯設備及內襯管件需求同步增溫， 而中國記
憶體廠及化學品廠亦持續擴充，下單動能有所增溫。公司於 2025 因市況需求不
佳，為維持稼動率，彰濱廠須承接經統包工程公司發包之低毛利訂單，而嘉興廠也
須面對中國同業削價競爭，影響毛利率表現，隨著市況於2026 大幅改善，除在手
訂單金額顯著回升外，彰濱廠及嘉興廠皆可挑單生產，產品組合預期將逐步好轉，
有利其毛利率逐步回升。 
2H26 產能達滿載，獲利有望持續增溫：展望 2H26，因客戶需求強勁，公司彰濱
廠及嘉興廠於 7M26 已達滿載水準，受客戶拉貨時點影響，目前工廠產值大於公
告營收表現，隨著客戶陸續提貨，應可見上品營收持續增溫。公司先前彰濱廠槽車
約當產能約為月產能 80 台，但隨著 4M26 擴產完畢，及公司持續招工補充人力，
公司產能已提升至月產能 140 台，因預期在手訂單金額將持續增加，公司規劃將
於 2027 年再擴充至月產能 170 台，嘉興廠人力亦提升約 10-12%，以因應客戶強
勁需求。而先前承接的低毛利訂單仍有部分尚未出貨完畢，但因營收比重持續下
降，預期產品組合應持續改善，亦有利毛利率及獲利表現回升。 
營運轉折向上，投資建議上調至買進：展望後市，台灣化學品廠及中國記憶體、化
學品廠擴建，推升對上品氟素樹脂產品需求，上品在手訂單金額顯著回升，公司產
能達滿載，隨著低毛利訂單陸續出貨完畢，產品組合改善，毛利率及獲利表現應可
見回升，觀察上品原料 PTFE、PFA 供需仍緊，報價趨勢於近年逐步走升，然上品
屬原料商 Chemours、Daikin 首要供應廠商，可確保原料供應穩定，又因上品為
氟素樹脂設備龍頭廠商，技術優於日本同業，故可向下游順利轉嫁原料成本漲幅。
研究部先前下調上品之投資評等，主要係因接單表現不佳，在手訂單金額下滑，部
分產品交期延後，公司須承接低毛利訂單填補產能，壓抑獲利表現，然近期已見在
手訂單金額回升，嘉興廠及彰濱廠皆達滿載水準，並可挑單生產改善產品組合，應
有利公司評價回升。研究部預估公司 2026 營收 55.86 億元（+18.2% YoY），毛
利率 36.09%，稅後淨利 11.4 億元 （+40.1% YoY） ，稅後EPS 14.3 元，預估2027
營收 72.71 億元 （+12.3% YoY） ， 毛利率39.29%， 稅後淨利17.28 億元 （+51.1% 
YoY），稅後 EPS 21.6 元，參考歷史 PER 區間約位於 10-20X，以 2027 年預估
EPS，給予 16X PER 評價，目標價 345 元，投資建議上調至買進。

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 23 日

1 群益投顧 
中磊 (5388  TT) 發表 50G PON ONU ， 支 援 對 稱 、 不 對 稱 傳 輸 模 式 ， 也 與GPON、XGS -PON
技 術 共 存 ， 降 低 營 運 商 全 面 汰 換 既 有局端 網路 設備 的 需 求 ， 也 有 助 於 有 線 電 視 業 者 逐
步 由 混 合 光 纖 同 軸 網 路 轉 向 更 深 入 的 光 纖 部 署 ， 除 此 之 外 ， 中 磊 也 規 劃 結 合 即 將 推 出
的 Wi -Fi 8 閘 道 器 ， 將 高 速 光 纖 連 線 延 伸 至 用 戶 端 無 線 網 路 ， 擴 大 家 用 與 商 用 網 通 設
備的應用範圍 ； 展 望 後 勢 ，受 客 戶 需 求 帶 動 ， 多 樣 乘 載 新 技 術 的 新 產 品 在2026 年 陸 續
出 貨 ， 由 於 公 司 持 續 追 買 所 需 的 半 導 體 零 組 件 ， 加 上2H26 有 多 樣 新 產 品 上 市 ， 預 期
營 收 、 獲 利 重 回 成 長 軌 道 ， 維 持Buy 投資建議 。

研 究 員 ： 劉 宜 和   
yiho.liu@capital.com.tw

中磊 發表 50G PON ONU ：  
中磊 發表 符合 ITU -T G.9804.3 標準 50G PON  ONU ， 隨 著 電 信 網 路 持 續 朝 向
更 高 頻 寬 需 求 發 展，50G PON 為 XGS -PON 之 後 的 新 一 代 光 纖 接 取 技 術，可
應用於住宅寬頻、企業網路、行動回傳及有線電視寬頻等市場， 該設備 可
支援下 載 50Gbps 、上 傳 25Gbps 的 非 對 稱 模 式 ， 以 及 上 下傳 均為 50Gbps 的
對 稱 模 式 ，並 與 GPON 、 XGS -PON 技 術 共 存 ， 降 低 營 運 商 全 面 汰 換 既 有局
端 網路 設備 的需求，也有助於有線電視業者逐步由混合光纖同軸網路轉向
更 深 入 的 光 纖 部 署，除 此 之 外，中 磊 也 規 劃 結 合 即 將 推 出 的 Wi -Fi 8 閘 道 器，
將高速光纖連線延伸至用戶端無線網路，擴大家用與商用 網通 設備的應用
範圍。  
展望 3Q26 ， 受 惠 於 終 端 網 路 技 術 升 級 ， 多 樣 換 機 潮 持 續 發 動 ， 有 線 電 視 廠
商為因應 10G PON 競 爭 ， 加 大 升 級 DOCSIS4.0 力 道 ， Cable Modem 出貨量
持 續 成 長 ； 企 業 級 網 通 品 牌 商 為 因 應 企 業 內 大 增 的AI 應 用 流 量 ， 大 量 導 入
WiFi -7 增 加 網 路 吞 吐 量 ； 北 美 行 動 網 路 營 運 商 利 用 自 家 毫 米 波 頻 段 切 入 企
業 市 場 ， 毫 米 波 5G FWA 新 專 案 放 量 出 貨 ， 推 動 新 機 出 貨 ， 預 估3Q26 營收
210.54 億 元 ，QoQ+2.14% ， 稅 後 淨 利 5.52 億 元 ，QoQ+3.01% ，EPS1.84 元 。 
展望 2026 年，雖 然 DRAM 成 本 大 幅 上 揚，但 因 主 要 客 戶 多 為 電 信 商 與 有 線
電 視 營 運 商，網 通 CPE 是 收 取 服 務 月 費 的 基 礎，不 會 因 DRAM 、NAND Flash
成本上漲而失去需求，加上新規格高速產品如： 5G FWA 、 10G PON 、
DOCSIS4.0 皆 已 搭 載 WiFi -7， 以 及 企 業 級 網 通 設 備 大 量 導 入 WiFi -7， 進 一
步 推 升 產 品 單 價 ， ， 預 估 2026 年 營 收 797.89 億 元 ， YoY+47.76% ，預估稅
後淨利 19.63 億 元 ， YoY+63.18% ， EPS6. 24 元 。  
受 客 戶 需 求 帶 動 ， 多 樣 乘 載 新 技 術 的 新 產 品 在2026 年 陸 續 出 貨 ， 由 於 公 司
持 續 追 買 所 需 的 半 導 體 零 組 件 ， 加 上 2H26 有 多 樣 新 產 品 上 市 ， 預 期 營 收 、
獲 利 重 回 成 長 軌 道 ， 維 持 Buy 投 資 建 議 。

### Page 3

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 23 日

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投資評等 係 「 首 次 」 給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時 ， 其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較 3 個
月目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為三個等級，定義如
上 。 爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」 及 「 買 進 」 均 有 upside 目 標 價 。 兩 者 差 別 僅 在 於 ， 首 次 評 等
時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「買進」及「強力買進」之投資評等，就是責任的
開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及 股 價 變 化 ，
直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ，
研 判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。

6667 信紘科 (TRUSVAL)

報告日期： 2026/10/05            研究員：王蕎程 (Joe Wang) 
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要: 
 訂單滿載與高能見度：信紘科現有訂單達 230 億元，受惠於
AI 與半導體擴建潮，廠務設備與二次配工程訂單滿載，能見度
看至 2028 年。  
 大型統包與海外擴張：業務從單一工程升級至數十億級大型機
電統包，並結合策略夥伴進軍美、日、德、新等海外市場，帶
動營收成長。  
 綠色永續與持續收益：成立專責子公司銳源環境科技，聚焦廢
液處理與資源循環，將一次性工程建置延伸至維修與代操等持
續性收入。

一. 高科技建廠需求強勁，1H26 稅後 EPS 達 7.70 元 
信紘科以特氣特化廠務供應設備系統與二次配裝機工程為主要業
務，近年營運重心與服務範疇持續升級至二次配統包以及機電統
包工程。2025 年高科技產業營收達 59.45 億元，占整體營收高達 
93.56%；2026 年第二季高科技產業營收仍占絕大多數，半導體與
高科技建廠已成為公司最核心且穩定的營收來源

2026 年上半年營收達 32.19 億元；營業毛利 7.49 億元， 毛利率 
23.25% ；營業利益 3.55 億元，稅後淨利 3.79 億元，累計 EPS 達 
7.70 元。目前公司現有在手訂單高達 230 億元，訂 單能見度已直
達 2028 年，整體營運維持強勁成長動能。

二.半導體擴建為主要成長動能，工程與統包範疇擴大 
在廠務與工程服務部分，信紘科目前量產與工程實績包含特氣特
化廠務供應設備、系統建置及二次配管線裝機工程。為進一步提
升單案規模與營運效能，公司將服務領域延伸至二次配統包與大
型機電統包（GC/EPC），使專案金額邁入數十億級規模。後續業
務擴張重點，亦包含綠色製造與循環經濟，於 2025 年 12 月成
立專責子公司「銳源環境科技」，聚焦廢液處理與資源循環，將
一次性建置工程延伸至維修、營運代操及再生產品銷售等多元商
業模式等長期持續性收入。

目前主要服務據點皆配合客戶全球擴產需求建置，公司在台灣擁
有苗栗、台中、台南等據點，憑藉完整的本土供應鏈與人力調度，
能就近支援各大晶圓廠新建、擴建與改建之即時需求（主系統工

方面，信紘科已設立日本、美國及泰國子公司，並積極結合策略

全球預估有近 20 座晶圓廠同步施工，且先進製程新建廠之工程

型工程，將為公司帶來長期龐大之承攬動能。

三. 預估 2026 / 2027 年稅後 EPS 14.76 / 16.83 元 
預估 2026 全年營收 71.17 億元，YoY +12.0%；營業毛利 16.41 
億元，YoY +27.1%，毛利率 23.06%；營業利益 7.92 億元，稅後
純益 7.57 億元，稅後 EPS 14.76 元。展望 2027 年，受惠全球
半導體廠與先進封裝建廠需求熱、在手 230 億元訂單高能見度，
加上大型 GC 統包工程持續認列與海外業務擴張，預計 2027 年
營收 78.29 億元，YoY +10.0%，營業毛利 18.18 億元，毛利率 
23.22% ，營業利益 9.76 億元，稅後純益 8.29 億元，稅後 EPS 為 
16.83 元。

四. 投資建議為「買進」，目標價 330 元 
綜合考量 (1)先進製程相關半導體需求維持強勁 (2) 公司可參與
的工程範圍擴大至海外及機電統包等大型工程，並觀察歷史及同
業本益比區間 ， 目前本益比 16 倍評價偏低 ， 故投資建議為「買進」，
目標價 330 元（20x 2027EPS）。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
信紘科技（Trusval Technology，股票代號：6667）成立於 1995 年，
深耕高科技製造供應鏈布局，並由既有廠務供應設備系統服務，
逐步拓展至機電統包、建廠總承攬及整合型工程解決方案。目前
業務範疇涵蓋廠務設備、製程二次配裝機服務，以及高科技廠房
整合建置與機電統包工程，可依客戶建廠及擴產需求，提供從工
程規劃、設計、採購、施工、測試驗證至系統整合與專案管理的
一站式服務。憑藉跨工種整合、精密製程系統建置及大型專案管
理能力，公司持續提升高附加價值工程案件比重，協助客戶縮短
建廠時程、確保工程品質並提升營運效率。因應全球淨零轉型與
資源循環需求，信紘科技亦積極發展製程機能水設備、廢液處理
與循環經濟解方、資源零廢中心建置等業務，將工程整合能力延
伸至綠色製造與永續基礎建設。

台灣為公司最核心且成熟的基本盤，憑藉超過 30 年高科技產物
工程的深厚經驗，累積了卓越的技術能力與執行實力；得益於台
灣完整的高標準供應鏈以及敏捷的人力與材料調度，能高效支援
客戶新建、擴建及改建廠需求，加上工程需求穩定且執行週期較
短（主系統約 6 至 8 個月，二次配約 4 至 6 個月），構築了
公司長期穩健的營業基礎。

為打造未來成長新引擎，公司順應客戶海外建廠的全球布局與緊
湊時程，將成熟的工程能力輸出海外，信紘科不僅以既有產物工
程為切入點，逐步擴展至純水、廢水、無塵室及機電等領域以擴
大單一案件服務範疇，在非美日等新興區域更結合策略夥伴共同
承攬；同時，公司統包能力持續升級，從早期高技術門檻的二次
配工程，拓展至跨系統二次配統包，進而邁向承接大規模、高整
合度的跨國 GC 與工程管理角色，透過整合台外資源提升接單層
級與案件金額，全面貼近客戶全球建廠需求。

二.產業趨勢概況 
市場概況 
A.半導體產業概況  
半導體產業為全球數位經濟之核心基礎建設，近年在人工智慧
（AI）、高效能運算（HPC）及邊緣運算應用加速普及帶動下，產
業需求結構出現顯著變化。先進製程與高密度運算晶片需求持續
提升，使晶圓廠投資動能自庫存調整週期中快速復甦，並進入新
一輪資本支出擴張階段。 根據國際半導體產業協會（SEMI）2025
年發布之預測，全球 300mm 晶圓廠設備支出於 2026 年至 2028 年
間累計將達約 3,740 億美元，其中 2025 年單年度支出將首次突破
1,000 億美元水準。顯示全球半導體建廠投資已由短期循環性波
動，逐步轉為結構性成長態勢。另依 Deloitte（2025）預估，全球
半導體銷售額有機會於 2030 年前後突破 1 兆美元規模，反映產業
長期需求動能仍具成長潛力。

B.半導體產業 ESG 趨勢 
隨淨零排放與國際永續規範逐步落實，ESG 已由合規要求轉為半
導體供應鏈的重要競爭要素。依 SNS Insider（ 2025）預估，全球
半導體廢液處理市場將由 2024 年的 27.2 億美元成長至 2032 年的
49.2 億美元，其中亞太地區受惠於晶圓廠持續擴建與永續轉型，
成長動能可望高於全球平均。在此趨勢下，半導體廠務環保技術
逐步朝 「資源再生、能源轉化、源頭減量」三大方向發展。首先，
硫酸、氨氮廢水及有機溶劑等製程廢液，正由傳統末端處理轉向
回收再生，經純化後重新作為工業級或電子級原料使用，提高資
源循環率並降低廢棄物處置需求。其次，有機廢液處理逐步導入
Waste-to-Energy 技術，將廢液轉化為燃料或能源使用， 以降低外購
能源需求與碳排放。

此外，先進製程亦更加重視源頭減量，透過綠色化學品替代與先
進流體製程，降低強酸、強鹼及水資源使用量。隨 3nm 以下製程
持續發展，兼具降低化學品耗用、廢水負荷與營運成本效益的技
術，導入需求可望提升。 
整體而言，未來半導體廠務市場將由單純設備供應，逐步轉向整

進入傳統旺季且 2027 年動能可期 
 近期營運：時序將進入傳統旺季，預估九月營收跳增，4Q26 進入旺
季，預估出貨量 QoQ 與 YoY 雙增，預估 4Q26 營收 54.4 億元，
+18.77% QoQ，+9.91% YoY，營益率隨稼動率提升且有規模提升所
帶來的營運槓桿效益提升，預估 EPS 1.51 元；2026 年 EPS 5.02 元，
以過往配息率 70%計算，推估現金股利 3.5 元，殖利率超過 5.5%。 
 未來展望：2026 年 ADIDAS 持平、NIKE 仍在調整中、Brooks 穩
定、Asics 持續面臨越南場招工不易影響出貨，預估 2026 年出貨量
約 3700~3800 萬雙。2027 年隨著印尼新廠放量且越南新廠完工 ， 產
能效益有望在 2H27 顯現，且搭配奧運前一年拉貨效應，2027 年有
望恢復增長態勢，我們預估 2027 年出貨量 4200~4300 萬雙。 
 產能效益在 2027 年顯現：印尼廠已於 2H25 開線，惟目前單月出
貨量仍未顯著提升。印尼一期目標年產能為 600 萬雙，未來將陸續
新增非美客戶投入產線，屆時印尼廠出貨增溫。越南新廠方面，目前
已有一棟廠房完工，預計 1Q27 試產，2H27 放量，屆時 2027 年印
尼與越南產能投放效益顯現，將為集團帶來新的營運動能。 
 2027 年在 ADIDAS 穩健成長、NIKE 持平；Brooks、Asics 出貨持續
放量，預估出貨量雙位數成長達 4300~4400 萬雙，不考慮匯率波動
影響下 ， 預估2027 年營收 229.76 億元(+10.43% YoY)， 隨著稼動率
提升且新廠效益逐步顯現 ， 毛利率有望提升至 18~19%間， 預估營業
利益 17.25 億元，營業利益率 7.51%，預估 EPS 7.06 元。 
 調升評等至買進 ：預估 2026 年 EPS 5.02 元、2027 年 EPS 7.06 元。
考量營運谷底已過，長期投資買浮現，且時序即將進入旺季、 2027
年營運恢復增長，股價下檔風險有限，故調升評等至買進，目標價
98 元。

志強為全球足球鞋代工領導廠
商，全球市占率 20%以上

志強為全球足球鞋代工領導廠商，全球市占率 20%以上 
志強-KY 於 2000 年開始進入運動鞋代工領域，以營收佔比來看，2024 年運動
鞋佔 84.81%、休閒鞋 13.8%以及其他 1.39%。以生產基地來看，越南為主要生
產基地，佔出貨比重約2/3、其次為柬埔寨，共有六個廠區，分別為志雄(越南)、
志中(越南)、志寧(越南)、大華(越南)、大河(越南)以及志寨(柬埔寨)，合計年產
能約 4500 萬雙。志強-KY 四大品牌客戶為 Nike、Adidas、Brooks 以及 Asics，
1H26 ADIDAS 佔營收比重約 60.5~61%、NIKE 24~25%、Brooks 7~7.5%以及
ASICS 5.5~6%。2023 年受到客戶庫存調整影響 ， 出貨量降至3039 萬雙 ，2024
年出貨量達 3471 萬雙，YOY+14.21%，2025 年出貨量接近 3950 萬雙，2026
年受到全球消費景氣不明朗且主要客戶下單趨於謹慎影響，預估出貨量衰退 ，
預估 2026 年出貨量約 3700~3800 萬雙。

志強現有生產基地

Source ：Company Data 、Masterlink

時序即將進入旺季，九月營收跳
增，預估 3Q26 EPS 0.8 4 元；

What’s New： 
時序即將進入旺季，九月營收跳增，預估3Q26 EPS 0.84 元；4Q26 營運向上，
預估 2026 年 EPS 5.02 元 
2Q26 受到印尼以及葡萄牙兩個新廠開始出貨 ， 初期仍在負毛利階段且印尼有長
假效應影響 ，2Q26 毛利率 17%， 營業利益率5.7%， 稅後歸屬母公司淨利潤2.93
億元，EPS 1.47 元，1H26 EPS 2.67 元。3Q26 進入傳統製鞋淡季，預期出貨
量 QOQ 下滑 ，YOY 小增， 預估營收45.80 億元，-15.63% QoQ，+10.54% YoY，
營益率受淡季影響，預估小幅向下，預估 EPS 0.84 元。然就月趨勢來看，時序
即將進入傳統旺季，預估九月營收 將跳增，4Q26 進入傳統旺季，預估出貨量
QoQ 與 YoY 雙增，我們預估 4Q26 營收 54.4 億元，+18.77% QoQ ，+9.91% 
YoY，營益率隨稼動率提升且有規模提升所帶來的營運槓桿效益， 預估本業獲利
3.48 億元，營業利益率 6.4%，以現行股本 20.01 億元計，EPS 1.51 元；預估
2026 年 EPS 5.02 元，以過往配息率 70%計算，推估現金股利 3.5 元，殖利率
超過 5.5%。

出貨量(千)

時序即將進入旺季，出貨動能增
溫，2027 年出貨恢復增長態勢 
 時序即將進入旺季，出貨動能增溫，2027 年出貨恢復增長態勢 
志強-KY 年產量約 4000~4300 萬雙，2019 年集團出貨量為 4100 萬雙，以足球
鞋為主力，2019 年足球鞋出貨量 2100 萬雙，全球市占率 21.03%，2019 年佔
Nike 足球鞋 35%、佔 Adidas 足球鞋的 38%。2020 年受到疫情影響，出貨量降
至 3300 萬雙 ，YoY 衰退 24%， 其中運動鞋衰退28%、 休閒鞋小幅衰退2.74%。
2021 年受到越南疫情導致停工衝擊，2021 年出貨量約 3344 萬雙，與 2020 年
約略持平。2022 年，在疫情衝擊下供應商大者仍大的趨勢更加明顯，上半年品
牌對於產能的需求殷切，終端需求面隨著歐美全面開放品牌端需求持續暢旺，
爾後 4Q22 開始進入庫存調整，2022 年全年出貨量為 4269 萬雙。2023 年品牌
客戶持續庫存調整，2023 年出貨量約 3039 萬雙。2024 年隨著客戶庫存調整完
畢且通路庫存持續去化，出貨量 3471 萬雙。2025 年受到關稅因素干擾，品牌
對於 2H25 下單趨於謹慎，2025 年出貨量約 3950 萬雙，其中 ADIDAS 維持
雙 位 數 成 長 、NIKE 趨 於 保 守 且 Brooks 及 Asics 出 貨 高 成 長 。2026 年
ADIDAS 持平、NIKE 仍在調整中，預估中~高個位數衰退；Brooks 穩定、Asics 
持續面臨越南場招工不 易影響，導致出貨量無法顯著放量，預估 志強全年出貨
量中個位數衰退 ， 就趨勢上來看 ，1Q26 與 2Q26 均維持在 940~950 萬雙水準，
1H26 出貨量 1890 萬雙 、2H26 YOY 持平到小增， 預估全年出貨量約3700~3800
萬雙。2027 年隨著印尼新廠放量且越南新廠完工 ， 產能效益有望在2H27 顯現，
且搭配奧運前一年拉貨效應，2027 年有望恢復增長態勢，我們預估 2027 年出
貨量 4200~4300 萬雙，其中 ADIDAS 穩健、NIKE 持平；Brooks、Asics 出貨
持續放量。

### Page 3

3【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
結論一：未來三年矽晶圓受AI帶動，需求成長遠高於供給成長
資料來源：SEMI、福邦投顧預估
• 議價權已轉回上游。2026/7美光與環球晶簽5億美元、10年期策略長約；客戶庫存去化進入尾聲，LTA循
環位置在復甦中段。
• 供給端受五大廠寡占＋擴產紀律約束，年增率長期僅1~3%（僅2017-18、2021擴產潮達6~8%）；需求端
2023-24連兩年衰退後，2025年+5.8%回升，2026-28年在AI帶動下估連三年+9~12%，供給無彈性遇上需求
高成長，是本輪缺口的核心結構。
• 價格與供需同步驗證：12吋ASP年均自2025年63美元落底，隨缺口擴大2027/2028年估達101/172美元。

### Page 4

4【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
結論二：預期矽晶圓2027年將供不應求，價格持續向上
資料來源：SEMI、各公司法說會，福邦投顧整理
• 量的復甦：SEMI統計2025年全球矽晶圓出貨面積12,973 MSI（+5.8%）終結連兩年衰退；2Q26出貨3,573 MSI
、年增7.4%、季增9.1%，創14季新高；SEMI預估2028年達15,485 MSI改寫歷史新高。需求增速遠高於供給放
量進程，研究員預估2027下半年正式轉為供不應求，2028年缺口約8%。
• 價格已經落地：2026/7信越、SUMCO、環球晶三大廠同步發出漲價函，12吋常規邏輯晶圓漲5~8%、AI/HPC
高階晶圓漲18~22%；信越7月法說指各家12吋已滿載、產業進入產能受限狀態，新一輪LTA將反映成本上漲，
價格上行趨勢確立。

### Page 5

矽晶圓為寡占、高壁壘的上游材料

### Page 6

6【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
矽晶圓位居半導體最上游，台灣連續16年為最大材料市場
資料來源：SEMI、TrendForce，福邦投顧整理
• 矽晶圓是所有晶片製造的起點與地基，約占晶圓製造材料22.9%、居各類材料之首，為需求量最大的半導體材料。台灣是全
球材料重心：2025年台灣半導體材料市場217億美元、占全球732億美元約29%，連續16年全球之冠（SEMI，2026）；
SUMCO銷售額34.7%來自台灣。台灣在中游（環球晶全球第三）與下游（台積電代工市占72.3%，1Q26創新高）均居要角，
上下游完整。
上游:原料與設備 中游:晶圓製造 下游:應用與代工
72.3%

### Page 7

7【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
五道工序中拉晶定生死，資本與技術雙密集
資料來源：國信證券、國泰海通，福邦投顧整理
• 產業具三大特性：(1)資本密集——12吋線每10萬片/月產能投資約數十億美元；(2)技術密集——12吋拋光片TTV<0.1μm、表
面雜質低於百億分之一；(3)強週期＋寡占——景氣循環約3~4年、擴產週期2年以上，五大廠藉多輪併購形成CR5逾90%格局
，環球晶即為併購成長典範（2012年Covalent、2016年Topsil與SunEdison，市占由第六升至第三）。

### Page 9

9【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
產品以拋光片與磊晶片為主力，SOI單價達4~5倍
資料來源：國信證券、福邦投顧整理
• 矽晶圓依加工深度分為研磨片、拋光片、退火片、磊晶片（外延片）與SOI。拋光片是基準產品，可直接
投產記憶體與功率元件，也是其他高階產品的襯底；磊晶片附加價值高，信越即以磊晶片為主力；SOI具
低功耗、抗輻射優勢，單價為一般矽晶圓的4~5倍，其中RF-SOI（5G射頻前端）占約60%。環球晶為少數
具備全產品線（含SOI、SiC、GaN）的廠商，其美國密蘇里廠即鎖定SOI利基市場。
表2、矽晶圓產品種類比較
種類 製程 用途／特點 價格水準
拋光片 研磨片＋CMP化學機械拋光 記憶體、功率元件；退火／磊晶／SOI之襯底 基準
退火片 拋光片經氫氣／氬氣高溫熱處理 去除表面氧、提高晶體完整性；先進製程用 略高於拋光片1.2倍
磊晶片 拋光片上CVD氣相生長單晶矽層 CPU/GPU、CIS、PMIC、IGBT；缺陷密度更低 高於拋光片1.5~2倍
SOI 氧化埋層＋鍵合／Smart-Cut RF射頻(60%)、功率、車用、星載晶片 約4~5倍

假片／再生片 晶棒次級部位／回收再拋光 設備暖機測試；N5每10萬片正片配約6萬片擋控
片 約4~5折以下

### Page 12

12【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
12吋為絕對主流，占出貨面積76%且持續提升
資料來源：SEMI、Omdia、國盛證券，福邦投顧整理
• 尺寸大型化是產業長期趨勢：12吋出貨面積占比自2000年
1.7%升至2024年76.4%、2025年約77%，8吋加12吋合計超
過96%。12吋面積為8吋的2.25倍、可用率約2.5倍，單位成
本優勢明顯，先進製程設備亦全數針對12吋開發。
• 應用分界在90奈米：90奈米以下（手機SoC、CPU/GPU、
記憶體、AI晶片）用12吋；90奈米以上成熟製程（PMIC
、車用MCU、功率、CIS）以8吋為主；6吋以下則轉向功
率元件與SiC。Siltronic預估矽晶圓長期需求CAGR 5~6%，
成長動能全部來自12吋。

• 五大廠擴產態度分歧：僅環球晶大規模新建，其餘以優化或小幅擴充為主
• 矽晶圓的寡占不是偶然：技術、認證、資金、設備規模四道門檻疊加，讓資金雄厚的新進者也難以速成
。最關鍵的是客戶認證——從送樣到主力供應商一般需5年以上（2年流片評估→1年測試片→1年低量正
片→B/C類→主力），且認證通過後客戶不輕易更換供應商，形成極強黏著度。

### Page 14

14【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
矽晶圓12吋為主流，記憶體為主要成長動能
資料來源：SEMI、材料世界網，福邦投顧整理
• 矽晶圓的終端應用依尺寸分層：12 吋占出貨面積 約75%、
是絕對主流。以晶片類型看，邏輯代工約 55%、DRAM／
HBM /3D NAND 等記憶體合計約 45%。記憶體廠的投片與
庫存決策因此主導矽晶圓的供需與價格，記憶體循環也成為
矽晶圓的領先指標
• 8 吋以 PMIC、車用 MCU 與功率元件為主，隨車用與消費性
回溫在 2H26 復甦；6 吋以下為分離式功率元件，SiC/GaN
亦以 6 吋為主流、逐步轉進 8 吋。AI 需求（HBM、先進邏
輯、CoWoS 矽中介層）全數落在 12 吋，是本輪增量來源。

19【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】

• AI對矽晶圓的拉動不只是「用量變多」：先進邏輯、HBM記憶體、CoWoS先進封裝三條路徑同時發力，而
這些需求在上一輪（2021~22年）矽晶圓廠規劃產能時多未被充分計入，造成本輪供需缺口比市場預期更大

• 2026年底缺口約8%：各大晶圓廠去庫存化中，12吋受HBM與先進邏輯製程逐步放量、8吋受PMIC需求回
溫帶動。
• 27H2轉供不應求、2028年缺口約8%，考量客戶庫存持續去化與有效產能爬坡率，全球新增晶圓廠與晶粒廠
有效產能（記憶體最多）於2H27陸續開出，2028年因晶圓廠與晶粒廠放量，缺口進一步擴大至8%。
• 未來產能三大瓶頸：(1)關鍵原物料——中國《兩用物項出口管制清單》限制鎵、鍺、稀土及高純度矽料出
口，為矽料生產、摻雜及外延片製造不可或缺（如信越約15%產能依賴此類上游原料）；(2)製程設備——

高階退火工藝所需氬氣及其製備設備關鍵部件受出口管制，已出現供應短缺；(3)人力吃緊——日韓廠商面
臨高齡化、技術人員短缺，難以調度足夠人力維持與擴大產能。

• HBM：產能2026/27/28年分別+120/100/150 kwpm，矽晶圓消耗量年增39%/23%/28%——2026年主要為DDR5產線轉型製造

三星YonginY1、SK海力士M15X與美光Idaho ID1，並額外計入長鑫存儲（CXMT）擴產貢獻（假設1:0.2）。
• NAND：擴產動能相對審慎，供給成長以「技術節點升級」為核心而非量能擴張，至2028年僅小幅增長50~80 kwpm（假設
200+層正片1:0.3）。

2026/7月漲價函落地，12吋常規預計漲5~8%、高階漲18~22%
資料來源：財通證券、鉅亨網、福邦投顧整理
• 價格轉折從預期變成事實：2026年7月信越、SUMCO、環球晶三大廠同步發出漲價函，自2026/7起生效，
適用2H26出貨與新簽訂單12吋常規邏輯晶圓調漲5~8%、AI/HPC專用高階晶圓（磊晶片為主）調漲
18~22%，為2022年以來首次全面調價；環球晶8/4法說會確認下半年啟動漲價、12吋產能幾乎滿載。
• 價格路徑：2025年ASP 0.879美元/平方英寸落底（量增價跌）→2026年結構性漲價（AI/先進製程/記憶體
先行、現貨H1+5~10%）→2027年供需比約104%、全面反轉。壓抑變數仍是中國8吋擴產，漲價呈「12吋
強、8吋溫和」的梯度分布。

ChipMOS Technologies 29 September 2026 ab 2
Forecast returns

Forecast stock return 60.0%
Market return assumption 6.3%
Forecast excess return 53.8%
Company Description
ChipMOS is a semiconductor back-end supplier with 45-50% of its sales from display driver
IC (~40% from TV and 40% from smartphone and IT and 20% from automotive), 35-40%
from memory (~40% DRAM, ~40% from specialty flash and ~20% from mainstream NAND)

and 10-15% from logic (TV SoC) and mixed signal IC (MEMS, PMIC).
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We have a Buy rating and a NT$166 price target, based on 23x average 2027-28E PE (vs. its
normalized 5-27x PE range). Our target multiple is in line with the average of memory and
driver IC OSAT peers.Downside risks include a weaker-than-expected tech end-market
demand recovery, growing China competition, failure to diversify into the SiPh/optical

component bumping business and a slower ramp of its logic testing business.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.
Important Information About Sustainable Investing Strategies: Sustainable investing strategies aim to consider and incorporate environmental, social and governance
(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.
Incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing considerations may inhibit the portfolio manager’s ability to participate in certain investment opportunities that otherwise

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.
